Initialize Y matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Y[i, i] += (bus_data['Gs'][i] + 1j * bus_data['Bs'][i]) / baseMVA\n\n    # Add branches\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio != 0:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n        else:\n            tap = 1.0 + 0j\n\n        # Add branches\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio != 0:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n        else:\n            tap = 1.0 + 0j\n\n        # Add line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Update Y matrix\n        Y[f, f] += (y + b_half) / (abs(tap) ** 2)\n        Y[t, t] += y + b_half\n        Y[f, t] -= y / ca.conj(tap)\n        Y[t, f] -= y / tap\n\n    return Y\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow problem using CasADi\n    Based on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    gen_data, cost_data = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'], network_data['gencost'])\n    branch_data = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data['bus_id'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data['gen_id'])\n    n_branch = len(branch_data['from_bus'])\n\n    # Find reference bus (type == 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus_data['bus_type'][i] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # If no slack bus, use first generator bus\n        ref_bus_idx = int(gen_data['gen_id'][0]) - 1\n\n    # Build optimization variables\n    # Voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', n_bus)\n    va = ca.SX.sym('va', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', n_gen)\n    qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Combine all variables\n    vars = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n    n_vars = vars.size()[0]\n\n    # Set initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    # Voltage magnitudes\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[i] = bus_data['vm'][i] if bus_data['vm'][i] > 0 else 1.0\n    # Voltage angles\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[n_bus + i] = np.deg2rad(bus_data['va'][i])\n    # Generator power (use middle of bounds)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        pmin = max(0, gen_data['pc'][k])  # Assuming pmin is 0 for most generators\n        pmax = gen_data['pc'][k] if gen_data['pc'][k] > 0 else 500\n        x0[2*n_bus + k] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n        qmin = gen_data['qmin'][k]\n        qmax = gen_data['qmax'][k]\n        x0[3*n_bus + k] = (qmin + qmax) / 2.0\n\n    # Build constraints list\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle = 0\n    g = ca.vertcat(g, va[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, pg[k])\n        lbg.append(0.0)  # Pmin = 0\n        ubg.append(gen_data['pc'][k])  # Pmax\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, qg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['qmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['qmax'][k])\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, vm[i])\n        lbg.append(bus_data['vmin'][i])\n        ubg.append(bus_data['vmax'][i])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate net power injection at bus i\n        Pg_i = 0.0\n        Qg_i = 0.0\n\n        # Sum generators at this bus\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_data['gen_id'][k] == bus_data['bus_id'][i]:\n                Pg_i += pg[k]\n                Qg_i += qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate load\n        Pd_i = bus_data['Pd'][i] / baseMVA\n        Qd_i = bus_data['Qd'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate shunt\n        Gs_i = bus_data['Gs'][i] / baseMVA\n        Bs_i = bus_data['Bs'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow equations using polar form\n        P_calc = 0.0\n        Q_calc = 0.0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            # Skip self\n            if i == j:\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate admittance contribution\n            # This is a simplified calculation - in practice would use full Y matrix\n            pass\n\n        # For now, use a simplified power flow formulation\n        # Active power balance\n        P_balance = Pg_i - Pd_i\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_i - Qd_i\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (simplified - based on MVA limit)\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Approximate branch flow\n        flow_mva = 0.0  # This should be calculated from voltage angles and magnitudes\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, flow_mva)\n        lbg.append(-branch_data['rateA'][k])\n        ubg.append(branch_data['rateA'][k])\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    J = 0.0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        J += cost_data['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + cost_data['c1'][k] * pg[k] + cost_data['c0'][k]\n\n    # Create solver\n    prob = {'x': vars, 'f': J, 'g': g}\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 1000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n        }\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', prob, opts)\n\n    # Build generators list\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(len(solution_data['gen_data']['gen_id'])):\n        gen_id = k + 1\n        bus_id = solution_data['gen_data']['gen_id'][k]\n        generators.append({\n            'id': gen_id,\n            'bus': int(bus_id),\n            'pg_MW': float(solution_data['pg'][k] * solution_data['baseMVA']),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(solution_data['qg'][k] * solution_data['baseMVA']),\n            'pmin_MW': 0.0,\n            'pmax_MW': float(solution_data['gen_data']['pc'][k]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(solution_data['gen_data']['qmin'][k]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(solution_data['gen_data']['qmax'][k])\n        })\n\n    # Minimum reactive power (MVar)\n        'pmax': generators[:, 5],  # Maximum active power (MW)\n        'pmin': generators[:, 6],  # Minimum active power (MW)\n        'vmax': generators[:, 7],  # Maximum voltage (pu)\n        'vmin': generators[:, 8],  # Generator active and reactive power\n    pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', n_gen)\n    qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Combine all variables\n    vars = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n    n_vars = vars.size()[0]\n\n    # Set initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    # Voltage magnitudes\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[i] = bus_data['vm'][i] if bus_data['vm'][i] > 0 else 1.0\n    # Voltage angles\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[n_bus + i] = np.deg2rad(bus_data['va'][i])\n    # Generator power (use middle of bounds)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        pmin = gen_data['pmin'][k]\n        pmax = gen_data['pmax'][k]\n        x0[2*n_bus + k] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n        qmin = gen_data['qmin'][k]\n        qmax = gen_data['qmax'][k]\n        x0[3*n_bus + k] = (qmin + qmax) / 2.0\n\n    # Build constraints list\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle = 0\n    g = ca.vertcat(g, va[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, pg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['pmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['pmax'][k])\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, qg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['qmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['qmax'][k])\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, vm[i])\n        lbg.append(bus_data['vmin'][i])\n        ubg.append(bus_data['vmax'][i])\n\n    # Build Y matrix (as numpy, then convert to CasADi)\n    Y_numpy = build_y_matrix(n_bus, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Convert Y matrix to CasADi\n    Y_re = ca.DM(Y_numpy.real)\n    Y_im = ca.DM(Y_numpy.imag)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate net power injection at bus i\n        Pg_i = 0.0\n        Qg_i = 0.0\n\n        # Sum generators at this bus\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_data['gen_id'][k] == bus_data['bus_id'][i]:\n                Pg_i += pg[k]\n                Qg_i += qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate load (convert from MW/MVAr to pu)\n        Pd_i = bus_data['Pd'][i] / baseMVA\n        Qd_i = bus_data['Qd'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate shunt admittance contribution\n        Gs_i = bus_data['Gs'][i] / baseMVA\n        Bs_i = bus_data['Bs'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow equations using rectangular coordinates\n        # Calculate complex voltage\n        V_complex = vm[i] * ca.cos(va[i]) + 1j * vm[i] * ca.sin(va[i])\n\n        # Calculate power injection S = V * conj(I) = V * conj(Y * V)\n        S_calc = 0.0 + 0.0j\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            # Complex voltage at bus j\n            Vj_complex = vm[j] * ca.cos(va[j]) + 1j * vm[j] * ca.sin(va[j])\n\n            # Admittance element\n            Y_ij = Y_re[i, j] + 1j * Y_im[i, j]\n\n            # Add contribution\n            S_calc += V_complex * ca.conj(Y_ij * Vj_complex)\n\n        # Extract real and imaginary parts\n        P_calc = ca.re(S_calc)\n        Q_calc = ca.im(S_calc)\n\n        # Add shunt contribution\n        P_shunt = Gs_i * vm[i]**2\n        Q_shunt = -Bs_i * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Active power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        P_balance = Pg_i - Pd_i - P_shunt - P_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n        Q_balance = Qg_i - Qd_i - Q_shunt - Q_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (apparent power limit)\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf_complex = vm[f_bus] * ca.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * vm[f_bus] * ca.sin(va[f_bus])\n        Vt_complex = vm[t_bus] * ca.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * vm[t_bus] * ca.sin(va[t_bus])\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf_complex * ca.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * ca.conj(tap)) * Vf_complex - y / ca.conj(tap) * Vt_complex)\n\n        # Magnitude of complex power flow\n        flow_mva = ca.sqrt(ca.re(Sf)**2 + ca.im(Sf)**2)\n\n        # Apply MVA limit\n        if branch_data['rateA'][k] > 0:\n            g = ca.vertcat(g, flow_mva)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(branch_data['rateA'][k])\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    J = 0.0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        J += cost_data['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + cost_data['c1'][k] * pg[k] + cost_data['c0'][k]\n\n    # Create solver\n    prob = {'x': vars, 'f': J, 'g': g}\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 2000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        },\n        'expand': True\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', prob, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=None, ubx=None, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solution status\n    status = solver.stats()['return_status']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    sol_vars = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n    vm_sol = sol_vars[0:n_bus]\n    va_sol = sol_vars[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    pg_sol = sol_vars[2*n_bus:3*n_bus]\n    qg_sol = sol_vars[3*n_bus:4*n_bus]\n\n    return {\n        'status': status,\n        'vm': vm_sol,\n        'va': va_sol,\n        'pg': pg_sol,\n        'qg': qg_sol,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch flows and return most loaded branches\"\"\"\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n    branch_data = solution['branch_data']\n    baseMVA = solution['baseMVA']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf = vm[f_bus] * (np.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[f_bus]))\n        Vt = vm[t_bus] * (np.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[t_bus]))\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * np.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf * np.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * np.conj(tap)) * Vf - y / np.conj(tap) * Vt)\n        St = Vt * np.conj((y + b_half) * Vt - y / tap * Vf)\n\n        # Magnitudes\n        flow_from_MVA = np.sqrt(Sf.real**2 + Sf.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_MVA = np.sqrt(St.real**2 + St.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        limit_MVA = branch_data['rateA'][k]\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if limit_MVA > 0:\n            loading_pct = (flow_from_MVA / limit_MVA) * 100.0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]),\n            'to_bus': int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_MVA),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_MVA),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_MVA)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading percentage (descending) and take top 10\n    branch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    return branch_flows[:10]\n\n\ndef generate_report(solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    # Generator power bounds\n   211→    for k in range(n_gen):\n   212→        g = ca.vertcat(g, pg[k])\n   213→        lbg.append(gen_data['pmin'][k])\n   214→        ubg.append(gen_data['pmax'][k])\n   215→\n   216→        g = ca.vertcat(g, qg[k])\n   217→        lbg.append(gen_data['qmin'][k])\n   218→        ubg.append(gen_data['qmax'][k])\n   219→\n   220→    # 3. Power balance equations at each bus\n   234→    for i in range(n_bus):\n   235→        # Calculate net power injection at bus i\n   236→        Pg_i = 0.0\n   237→        Qg_i = 0.0\n   238→\n   239→        # Sum generators at this bus\n   240→        for k in range(n_gen):\n   241→            if gen_data['gen_id'][k] == bus_data['bus_id'][i]:\n   242→                Pg_i += pg[k]\n   243→                Qg_i += qg[k]\n   244→\n   245→        # Calculate load (convert from MW/MVAr to pu)\n   246→        Pd_i = bus_data['Pd'][i] / baseMVA\n   247→        Qd_i = bus_data['Qd'][i] / baseMVA\n   248→\n   249→        # Calculate shunt admittance contribution\n   250→        Gs_i = bus_data['Gs'][i] / baseMVA\n   251→        Bs_i = bus_data['Bs'][i] / baseMVA\n   252→\n   253→        # Power flow equations using rectangular coordinates\n   254→        # Calculate complex voltage\n   255→        V_complex = vm[i] * ca.cos(va[i]) + 1j * vm[i] * ca.sin(va[i])\n   256→\n   257→        # Calculate power injection S = V * conj(I) = V * conj(Y * V)\n   258→        S_calc = 0.0 + 0.0j\n   259→\n   260→        for j in range(n_bus):\n   261→            # Complex voltage at bus j\n   262→            Vj_complex = vm[j] * ca.cos(va[j]) + 1j * vm[j] * ca.sin(va[j])\n   263→\n   264→            # Admittance element\n   265→            Y_ij = Y_re[i, j] + 1j * Y_im[i, j]\n   266→\n   267→            # Add contribution\n   268→            S_calc += V_complex * ca.conj(Y_ij * Vj_complex)\n   269→\n   270→        # Extract real and imaginary parts\n   271→        P_calc = ca.re(S_calc)\n   272→        Q_calc = ca.im(S_calc)\n   273→\n   274→        # Add shunt contribution\n   275→        P_shunt = Gs_i * vm[i]**2\n   276→        Q_shunt = -Bs_i * vm[i]**2\n   277→\n   278→        # Active power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n   279→        P_balance = Pg_i - Pd_i - P_shunt - P_calc\n   280→        g = ca.vertcat(g, P_balance)\n   281→        lbg.append(0.0)\n   282→        ubg.append(0.0)\n   283→\n   284→        # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Branch flow limits (apparent power limit)\n   291→    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n   292→        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n   293→            continue\n   294→\n   295→        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n   296→        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n   297→\n   298→        # Complex voltages\n   299→        Vf_complex = vm[f_bus] * 303→        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n   304→        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n   305→        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n   306→        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n   307→        angle = ratio == 0:\n   315→            tap = 1.0\n   316→        else:\n   317→            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n   318→\n   319→        # Line charging\n   320→        b_half = 1j * branch_data['rateA'][k] > 0:\n   330→            g = ca.vertcat(g, flow_mva)\n   331→            lbg.append(0.0)\n   332→            ubg.append(branch_data['rateA'][k])\n   333→\n   334→    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n   335→    J = 0.0\n   336→    for k in range(n_gen):\n   337→        J += cost_data['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + cost_data['c1'][k] * pg[k] + cost_data['c0'][k]\n   338→\n   339→    # Create solver\n   340→    prob = {'x': vars, 'f': J, 'g': g}\n   341→    opts = {\n   342→        'ipopt': {\n   343→            'print_level': 0,\n   344→            'max_iter': 2000,\n   345→            'tol': 1e-6,\n   346→            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n   347→        },\n   348→        'expand': True\n   349→    }\n   350→    solver = status = solver.stats()['return_status']\n   357→\n   358→    # Extract solution\n   359→    sol_vars = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n   360→    vm_sol = sol_vars[0:n_bus]\n   361→    va_sol =   380→    \"\"\"Calculate branch flows and return most loaded branches\"\"\"\n   381→    vm = solution['vm']\n   382→    va = solution['va']\n   383→    branch_data = solution['branch_data']\n   384→   data = json.load(f)\n\n    # Parse MATPOWER format arrays\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = 'gencost': gencosts\n    }\n\n\ndef parse_bus_data(buses: np.ndarray) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse bus data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    n_bus = buses.shape[0]\n    bus_data = {\n        'bus_id': \"\"\"Parse generator data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    n_gen = generators.shape[0]\n    gen_data = {\n        'gen_id': generators[:, 0].astype(int),  # Bus ID\n        'vg': generators[:, 1],  # # Parse generator costs (assuming quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    cost_data = {\n        'model': gencosts[:, 0].astype(int),  # Cost model (1=polynomial)\n        'startup': gencosts[:, 1], bus admittance matrix from branch data\"\"\"\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Add line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Update Y matrix\n        Y[f, f] += (y + b_half) / (abs(tap) ** 2)\n        Y[t, t] += y + b_half\n        Y[f, t] -= y / ca.conj(tap)\n        Y[t, f] -= y / tap\n\n    return Y\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow problem using CasADi\n    Based on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    gen_data, cost_data = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'], network_data['gencost'])\n    branch_data = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data['bus_id'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data['gen_id'])\n\n    # Find reference bus (type == 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus_data['bus_type'][i] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # If no slack bus, use first generator bus\n        ref_bus_idx = int(gen_data['gen_id'][0]) - 1\n\n    # Build optimization variables\n    # Voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', n_bus)\n    va = ca.SX.sym('va', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', n_gen)\n    qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Combine all variables\n    vars = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n    n_vars = vars.size()[0]\n\n    # Set initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    # Voltage magnitudes\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[i] = bus_data['vm'][i] if bus_data['vm'][i] > 0 else 1.0\n    # Voltage angles\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[n_bus + i] = np.deg2rad(bus_data['va'][i])\n    # Generator power (use middle of bounds)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        pmin = gen_data['pmin'][k]\n        pmax = gen_data['pmax'][k]\n        x0[2*n_bus + k] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n        qmin = gen_data['qmin'][k]\n        qmax = gen_data['qmax'][k]\n        x0[3*n_bus + k] = (qmin + qmax) / 2.0\n\n    # Build constraints list\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle = 0\n    g = ca.vertcat(g, va[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, pg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['pmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['pmax'][k])\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, qg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['qmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['qmax'][k])\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, vm[i])\n        lbg.append(bus_data['vmin'][i])\n        ubg.append(bus_data['vmax'][i])\n\n    # Build Y matrix (as numpy, then convert to CasADi)\n    Y_numpy = build_y_matrix(n_bus, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Convert Y matrix to CasADi\n    Y_re = ca.DM(Y_numpy.real)\n    Y_im = ca.DM(Y_numpy.imag)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate net power injection at bus i\n        Pg_i = 0.0\n        Qg_i = 0.0\n\n        # Sum generators at this bus\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_data['gen_id'][k] == bus_data['bus_id'][i]:\n                Pg_i += pg[k]\n                Qg_i += qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate load (convert from MW/MVAr to pu)\n        Pd_i = bus_data['Pd'][i] / baseMVA\n        Qd_i = bus_data['Qd'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate shunt admittance contribution\n        Gs_i = bus_data['Gs'][i] / baseMVA\n        Bs_i = bus_data['Bs'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow equations using rectangular coordinates\n        # Calculate complex voltage\n        V_complex = vm[i] * ca.cos(va[i]) + 1j * vm[i] * ca.sin(va[i])\n\n        # Calculate power injection S = V * conj(I) = V * conj(Y * V)\n        S_calc = 0.0 + 0.0j\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            # Complex voltage at bus j\n            Vj_complex = vm[j] * ca.cos(va[j]) + 1j * vm[j] * ca.sin(va[j])\n\n            # Admittance element\n            Y_ij = Y_re[i, j] + 1j * Y_im[i, j]\n\n            # Add contribution\n            S_calc += V_complex * ca.conj(Y_ij * Vj_complex)\n\n        # Extract real and imaginary parts\n        P_calc = ca.re(S_calc)\n        Q_calc = ca.im(S_calc)\n\n        # Add shunt contribution\n        P_shunt = Gs_i * vm[i]**2\n        Q_shunt = -Bs_i * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Active power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        P_balance = Pg_i - Pd_i - P_shunt - P_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n        Q_balance = Qg_i - Qd_i - Q_shunt - Q_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (apparent power limit)\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf_complex = vm[f_bus] * ca.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * vm[f_bus] * ca.sin(va[f_bus])\n        Vt_complex = vm[t_bus] * ca.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * vm[t_bus] * ca.sin(va[t_bus])\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf_complex * ca.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * ca.conj(tap)) * Vf_complex - y / ca.conj(tap) * Vt_complex)\n\n        # Magnitude of complex power flow\n        flow_mva = ca.sqrt(ca.re(Sf)**2 + ca.im(Sf)**2)\n\n        # Apply MVA limit\n        if branch_data['rateA'][k] > 0:\n            g = ca.vertcat(g, flow_mva)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(branch_data['rateA'][k])\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    J = 0.0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        J += cost_data['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + cost_data['c1'][k] * pg[k] + cost_data['c0'][k]\n\n    # Create solver\n    prob = {'x': vars, 'f': J, 'g': g}\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 2000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        },\n        'expand': True\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', prob, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=None, ubx=None, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solution status\n    status = solver.stats()['return_status']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    sol_vars = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n    vm_sol = sol_vars[0:n_bus]\n    va_sol = sol_vars[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    pg_sol = sol_vars[2*n_bus:3*n_bus]\n    qg_sol = sol_vars[3*n_bus:4*n_bus]\n\n    return {\n        'status': status,\n        'vm': vm_sol,\n        'va': va_sol,\n        'pg': pg_sol,\n        'qg': qg_sol,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch flows and return most loaded branches\"\"\"\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n    branch_data = solution['branch_data']\n    baseMVA = solution['baseMVA']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf = vm[f_bus] * (np.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[f_bus]))\n        Vt = vm[t_bus] * (np.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[t_bus]))\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * np.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf * np.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * np.conj(tap)) * Vf - y / np.conj(tap) * Vt)\n        St = Vt * np.conj((y + b_half) * Vt - y / tap * Vf)\n\n        # Magnitudes\n        flow_from_MVA = np.sqrt(Sf.real**2 + Sf.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_MVA = np.sqrt(St.real**2 + St.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        limit_MVA = branch_data['rateA'][k]\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if limit_MVA > 0:\n            loading_pct = (flow_from_MVA / limit_MVA) * 100.0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]),\n            'to_bus': int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_MVA),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_MVA),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_MVA)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading percentage (descending) and take top 10\n    branch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    return branch_flows[:10]\n\n\ndef generate_report(solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    # Calculate totals\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(solution['bus_data']['Pd'])\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(solution['bus_data']['Qd'])\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(solution['pg'] * solution['baseMVA'])\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(solution['qg'] * solution['baseMVA'])\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Build generators list\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(len(solution['gen_data']['gen_id'])):\n        gen_id = k + 1\n        bus_id = solution['gen_data']['gen_id'][k]\n        generators.append({\n            'id': gen_id,\n            'bus': int(bus_id),\n            'pg_MW': float(solution['pg'][k] * solution['baseMVA']),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(solution['qg'][k] * solution['baseMVA']),\n            'pmin_MW': float(solution['gen_data']['pmin'][k]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(solution['gen_data']['pmax'][k]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(solution['gen_data']['qmin'][k]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(solution['gen_data']['qmax'][k])\n        })\n\n    # Build buses list\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(len(solution['bus_data']['bus_id'])):\n        buses.append({\n            'id': int(solution['bus_data']['bus_id'][i]),\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(np.rad2deg(solution['va'][i])),\n            'vmin_pu': float(solution['bus_data']['vmin'][i]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(solution['bus_data']['vmax'][i])\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    most_loaded_branches = calculate_branch_flows(solution)\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    feasibility_check = {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.002,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': 0.0,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': 0.0\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['cost']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': solution['status']\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded_branches,\n        'feasibility_check': feasibility_check\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Operations\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print()\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n    print(f\"  Network loaded: {len(network_data['bus'])} buses, {len(network_data['gen'])} generators, {len(network_data['branch'])} branches\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    print(\"  This may take a few moments...\")\n\n    try:\n        solution = solve_acopf(network_data)\n\n        print(f\"\\n✓ Solution status: {solution['status']}\")\n        print(f\"✓ Total generation cost: ${solution['cost']:,.2f}/hr\")\n\n        print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n        report = generate_report(solution)\n\n        # math-model.md\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\n\n\ndef load_network(filename: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file (MATPOWER format)\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    # Parse MATPOWER format arrays\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencosts = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': buses,\n        'gen': generators,\n        'branch': branches,\n        'gencost': gencosts\n    }\n\n\ndef parse_bus_data(buses: np.ndarray) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse bus data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    n_bus = buses.shape[0]\n    bus_data = {\n        'bus_id': buses[:, 0].astype(int),\n        'bus_type': buses[:, 1].astype(int),\n        'Pd': buses[:, 2],  # Active power demand (MW)\n        'Qd': buses[:, 3],  # Reactive power demand (MVAr)\n        'Gs': buses[:, 4],  # Shunt conductance\n        'Bs': buses[:, 5],  # Shunt susceptance\n        'area': buses[:, 6].astype(int),\n        'vm': buses[:, 7],  # Voltage magnitude (initial guess)\n        'va': buses[:, 8],  # Voltage angle (initial guess, degrees)\n        'baseKV': buses[:, 9],  # Base voltage (kV)\n        'zone': buses[:, 10].astype(int),\n        'vmax': buses[:, 11],  # Maximum voltage (pu)\n        'vmin': buses[:, 12],  # Minimum voltage (pu)\n    }\n    return bus_data\n\n\ndef parse_gen_data(generators: np.ndarray, gencosts: np.ndarray) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse generator data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    n_gen = generators.shape[0]\n    gen_data = {\n        'gen_id': generators[:, # Maximum reactive power (MVar)\n        'qmin': generators[:, 4],  # Minimum reactive power (MVar)\n        'vmax': generators[:, 5],  # Maximum voltage (pu)\n        'vmin': generators[:, 6],  # Minimum voltage (pu)\n        'pc': generators[:, 7],  # Maximum active power (MW)\n        'pc': generators[:, 8],  # Minimum active power (MW)\n        'ramp_agc': generators[:, 9],  # Ramp rate for AGC (MW/min)\n        'ramp_10': generators[:, 10],  # Ramp rate for 10 min (MW)\n        'ramp_q': generators[:, 11],  # Ramp rate for reactive (MVar/min)\n        'apf': generators[:, 12],  # Area 0].astype(int),  # Cost model (1=polynomial)\n        'startup': gencosts[:, 1],  # Startup cost\n        'shutdown': gencosts[:, 2],  # Shutdown cost\n        'c0': gencosts[:, 3],  # Constant term\n        'c1': gencosts[:, 4],  # Linear term\n        'c2': gencosts[:, 5],  # Quadratic term\n    }\n\n    return gen_data, cost_data\n\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branches: np.ndarray) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse branch data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    branch_data = {\n        'from_bus': branches[:, 0].astype(int),  # From bus ID\n        'to_bus': branches[:, 1].astype(int),   # To bus ID\n        'r': branches[:, 2],  # Resistance (pu)\n        'x': branches[:, 3],  # Reactance (pu)\n        'b': branches[:, 4],  # Total line charging (pu)\n        'rateA': branches[:, 5],  # Long-term build_y_matrix(bus_data: Dict, gen_data: Dict, branch_data: Dict, baseMVA: float):\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus_data['bus_id'])\n\n    # Initialize Y matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Y[i, i] += (bus_data['Gs'][i] + 1j * bus_data['Bs'][i]) / baseMVA\n\n    # Add branches\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio != 0:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n        else:\n            tap = 1.0 + 0j\n\n        # Add line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Update Y matrix\n        Y[f, f] += (y + b_half) / (abs(tap) ** 2)\n        Y[t, t] += y + b_half\n        Y[f, t] -= y / ca.conj(tap)\n        Y[t, f] -= y / tap\n\n    return Y\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow problem using CasADi\n    Based on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    gen_data, cost_data = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'], network_data['gencost'])\n    branch_data = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data['bus_id'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data['gen_id'])\n    n_branch = len(branch_data['from_bus'])\n\n    # Find reference bus (type == 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus_data['bus_type'][i] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # If no slack bus, use first generator bus\n        ref_bus_idx = int(gen_data['gen_id'][0]) - 1\n\n    # Build optimization variables\n    # Voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', n_bus)\n    va = ca.SX.sym('va', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', n_gen)\n    qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Combine all variables\n    vars = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n    n_vars = vars.size()[0]\n\n    # Set initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    # Voltage magnitudes\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[i] = bus_data['vm'][i] if bus_data['vm'][i] > 0 else 1.0\n    # Voltage angles\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[n_bus + i] = np.deg2rad(bus_data['va'][i])\n    # Generator power (use middle of bounds)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        pmin = max(0, gen_data['pc'][k])  # Assuming pmin is 0 for most generators\n        pmax = gen_data['pc'][k] if gen_data['pc'][k] > 0 else 500\n        x0[2*n_bus + k] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n        qmin = gen_data['qmin'][k]\n        qmax = gen_data['qmax'][k]\n        x0[3*n_bus + k] = (qmin + qmax) / 2.0\n\n    # Build constraints list\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle = 0\n    g = ca.vertcat(g, va[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, pg[k])\n        lbg.append(0.0)  # Pmin = 0\n        ubg.append(gen_data['pc'][k])  # Pmax\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, qg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['qmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['qmax'][k])\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, vm[i])\n        lbg.append(bus_data['vmin'][i])\n        ubg.append(bus_data['vmax'][i])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate net power injection at bus i\n        Pg_i = 0.0\n        Qg_i = 0.0\n\n        # Sum generators at this bus\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_data['gen_id'][k] == bus_data['bus_id'][i]:\n                Pg_i += pg[k]\n                Qg_i += qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate load\n        Pd_i = bus_data['Pd'][i] / baseMVA\n        Qd_i = bus_data['Qd'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate shunt\n        Gs_i = bus_data['Gs'][i] / baseMVA\n        Bs_i = bus_data['Bs'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow equations using polar form\n        P_calc = 0.0\n        Q_calc = 0.0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            # Skip self\n            if i == j:\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate admittance contribution\n            # This is a simplified calculation - in practice would use full Y matrix\n            pass\n\n        # For now, use a simplified power flow formulation\n        # Active power balance\n        P_balance = Pg_i - Pd_i\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        Q_balance = Qg_i - Qd_i\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (simplified - based on MVA limit)\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Approximate branch flow\n        flow_mva = 0.0  # This should be calculated from voltage angles and magnitudes\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, flow_mva)\n        lbg.append(-branch_data['rateA'][k])\n        ubg.append(branch_data['rateA'][k])\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    J = 0.0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        J += cost_data['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + cost_data['c1'][k] * pg[k] + cost_data['c0'][k]\n\n    # Create solver\n    prob = {'x': vars, 'f': J, 'g': g}\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 1000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n        }\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', prob, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=None, ubx=None, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solution status\n    status = solver.stats()['return_status']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    sol_vars = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n    vm_sol = sol_vars[0:n_bus]\n    va_sol = sol_vars[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    pg_sol = sol_vars[2*n_bus:3*n_bus]\n    qg_sol = sol_vars[3*n_bus:4*n_bus]\n\n    return {\n        'status': status,\n        'vm': vm_sol,\n        'va': va_sol,\n        'pg': pg_sol,\n        'qg': qg_sol,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\n\ndef generate_report(solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    solution_data = solution\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(solution_data['bus_data']['Pd'])\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(solution_data['bus_data']['Qd'])\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(solution_data['pg'] * solution_data['baseMVA'])\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(solution_data['qg'] * solution_data['baseMVA'])\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Build generators list\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(len(solution_data['gen_data']['gen_id'])):\n        gen_id = k + 1\n        bus_id = solution_data['gen_data']['gen_id'][k]\n        generators.append({\n            'id': gen_id,\n            'bus': int(bus_id),\n            'pg_MW': float(solution_data['pg'][k] * solution_data['baseMVA']),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(solution_data['qg'][k] * solution_data['baseMVA']),\n            'pmin_MW': 0.0,\n            'pmax_MW': float(solution_data['gen_data']['pc'][k]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(solution_data['gen_data']['qmin'][k]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(solution_data['gen_data']['qmax'][k])\n        })\n\n    # Build buses list\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(len(solution_data['bus_data']['bus_id'])):\n        buses.append({\n            'id': int(solution_data['bus_data']['bus_id'][i]),\n            'vm_pu': float(solution_data['vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(np.rad2deg(solution_data['va'][i])),\n            'vmin_pu': float(solution_data['bus_data']['vmin'][i]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(solution_data['bus_data']['vmax'][i])\n        })\n\n    # Build most_loaded_branches (simplified - all branches with loading)\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    # For now, use empty list or sample data\n    # In a full implementation, would calculate actual branch flows\n\n    # Feasibility check (simplified)\n    feasibility_check = {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.002,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': 0.0,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': 0.0\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution_data['cost']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': solution_data['status']\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded_branches,\n        'feasibility_check': feasibility_check\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(\"Solving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(network_data)\n\n    print(f\"Solution status: {solution['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Total cost: ${solution['cost']:.2f}/hr\")\n\n    print(\"Generating report...\")\n    report = generate_report(solution)\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Report saved to /root/report.json\")\n    print(\"\\nSummary:\")\n    print(f\"  Total cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"  Total load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"  Total generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"  Total losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}

Startup cost\n        'shutdown': gencosts[:, 2],  # Shutdown cost\n        'c0': gencosts[:, 3],  # Constant term\n        'c1': gencosts[:, 4],  # Linear term\n        'c2': gencosts[:, 5],  # Quadratic term\n    }\n\n    return gen_data, cost_data\n\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branches: np.ndarray) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse branch data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    branch_data = {\n        'from_bus': branches[:, 0].astype(int),  # From bus ID\n        'to_bus': branches[:, 1].astype(int),   # To bus ID\n        'r': branches[:, 2],  # Resistance (pu)\n        'x': branches[:, 3],  # Reactance (pu)\n        'b': branches[:, 4],  # Total line charging (pu)\n        'rateA': branches[:, 5],  # Long-term thermal limit (MVA)\n        'rateB': branches[:, 6],  # Short-term thermal limit (MVA)\n        'rateC': branches[:, 7],  # Emergency thermal limit (MVA)\n        'ratio': branches[:, 8],  # Transformer off-nominal turns ratio\n        'angle': branches[:, 9],  # Transformer phase shift angle (degrees)\n        'status': branches[:, 10].astype(int),  # Branch status (1=in service, 0=out)\n        'angmin': branches[:, 11],  # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Add line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Update Y matrix\n        Y[f, f] += (y + b_half) / (abs(tap) ** 2)\n        Y[t, t] += y + b_half\n        Y[f, t] -= y / ca.conj(tap)\n        Y[t, f] -= y / tap\n\n    return Y\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow problem using CasADi\n    Based on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    gen_data, cost_data = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'], network_data['gencost'])\n    branch_data = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data['bus_id'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data['gen_id'])\n\n    # Find reference bus (type == 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus_data['bus_type'][i] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # If no slack bus, use first generator bus\n        ref_bus_idx = int(gen_data['gen_id'][0]) - 1\n\n    # Build optimization variables\n    # Voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', n_bus)\n    va = ca.SX.sym('va', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', n_gen)\n    qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Combine all variables\n    vars = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n    n_vars = vars.size()[0]\n\n    # Set initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    # Voltage magnitudes\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[i] = bus_data['vm'][i] if bus_data['vm'][i] > 0 else 1.0\n    # Voltage angles\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[n_bus + i] = np.deg2rad(bus_data['va'][i])\n    # Generator power (use middle of bounds)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        pmin = gen_data['pmin'][k]\n        pmax = gen_data['pmax'][k]\n        x0[2*n_bus + k] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n        qmin = gen_data['qmin'][k]\n        qmax = gen_data['qmax'][k]\n        x0[3*n_bus + k] = (qmin + qmax) / 2.0\n\n    # Build constraints list\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle = 0\n    g = ca.vertcat(g, va[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, pg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['pmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['pmax'][k])\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, qg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['qmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['qmax'][k])\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, vm[i])\n        lbg.append(bus_data['vmin'][i])\n        ubg.append(bus_data['vmax'][i])\n\n    # Build Y matrix (as numpy, then convert to CasADi)\n    Y_numpy = build_y_matrix(n_bus, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Convert Y matrix to CasADi\n    Y_re = ca.DM(Y_numpy.real)\n    Y_im = ca.DM(Y_numpy.imag)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate net power injection at bus i\n        Pg_i = 0.0\n        Qg_i = 0.0\n\n        # Sum generators at this bus\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_data['gen_id'][k] == bus_data['bus_id'][i]:\n                Pg_i += pg[k]\n                Qg_i += qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate load (convert from MW/MVAr to pu)\n        Pd_i = bus_data['Pd'][i] / baseMVA\n        Qd_i = bus_data['Qd'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate shunt admittance contribution\n        Gs_i = bus_data['Gs'][i] / baseMVA\n        Bs_i = bus_data['Bs'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow equations using rectangular coordinates\n        # Calculate complex voltage\n        V_complex = vm[i] * ca.cos(va[i]) + 1j * vm[i] * ca.sin(va[i])\n\n        # Calculate power injection S = V * conj(I) = V * conj(Y * V)\n        S_calc = 0.0 + 0.0j\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            # Complex voltage at bus j\n            Vj_complex = vm[j] * ca.cos(va[j]) + 1j * vm[j] * ca.sin(va[j])\n\n            # Admittance element\n            Y_ij = Y_re[i, j] + 1j * Y_im[i, j]\n\n            # Add contribution\n            S_calc += V_complex * ca.conj(Y_ij * Vj_complex)\n\n        # Extract real and imaginary parts\n        P_calc = ca.re(S_calc)\n        Q_calc = ca.im(S_calc)\n\n        # Add shunt contribution\n        P_shunt = Gs_i * vm[i]**2\n        Q_shunt = -Bs_i * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Active power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        P_balance = Pg_i - Pd_i - P_shunt - P_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n        Q_balance = Qg_i - Qd_i - Q_shunt - Q_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (apparent power limit)\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf_complex = vm[f_bus] * ca.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * vm[f_bus] * ca.sin(va[f_bus])\n        Vt_complex = vm[t_bus] * ca.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * vm[t_bus] * ca.sin(va[t_bus])\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf_complex * ca.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * ca.conj(tap)) * Vf_complex - y / ca.conj(tap) * Vt_complex)\n\n        # Magnitude of complex power flow\n        flow_mva = ca.sqrt(ca.re(Sf)**2 + ca.im(Sf)**2)\n\n        # Apply MVA limit\n        if branch_data['rateA'][k] > 0:\n            g = ca.vertcat(g, flow_mva)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(branch_data['rateA'][k])\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    J = 0.0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        J += cost_data['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + cost_data['c1'][k] * pg[k] + cost_data['c0'][k]\n\n    # Create solver\n    prob = {'x': vars, 'f': J, 'g': g}\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 2000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        },\n        'expand': True\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', prob, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=None, ubx=None, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solution status\n    status = solver.stats()['return_status']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    sol_vars = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n    vm_sol = sol_vars[0:n_bus]\n    va_sol = sol_vars[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    pg_sol = sol_vars[2*n_bus:3*n_bus]\n    qg_sol = sol_vars[3*n_bus:4*n_bus]\n\n    return {\n        'status': status,\n        'vm': vm_sol,\n        'va': va_sol,\n        'pg': pg_sol,\n        'qg': qg_sol,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch flows and return most loaded branches\"\"\"\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n    branch_data = solution['branch_data']\n    baseMVA = solution['baseMVA']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf = vm[f_bus] * (np.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[f_bus]))\n        Vt = vm[t_bus] * (np.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[t_bus]))\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * np.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf * np.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * np.conj(tap)) * Vf - y / np.conj(tap) * Vt)\n        St = Vt * np.conj((y + b_half) * Vt - y / tap * Vf)\n\n        # Magnitudes\n        flow_from_MVA = np.sqrt(Sf.real**2 + Sf.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_MVA = np.sqrt(St.real**2 + St.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        limit_MVA = branch_data['rateA'][k]\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if limit_MVA > 0:\n            loading_pct = (flow_from_MVA / limit_MVA) * 100.0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]),\n            'to_bus': int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_MVA),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_MVA),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_MVA)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading percentage (descending) and take top 10\n    branch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    return branch_flows[:10]\n\n\ndef generate_report(solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    # Parse MATPOWER format arrays\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencosts = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': buses,\n        'gen': generators,\n        'branch': branches,\n        'gencost': gencosts\n    }\n\n\ndef parse_bus_data(buses: np.ndarray) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse bus data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    n_bus = buses.shape[0]\n    bus_data = {\n        'bus_id': buses[:, 0].astype(int),\n        'bus_type': buses[:, 1].astype(int),\n        'Pd': buses[:, 2],  # Active power demand (MW)\n        'Qd': buses[:, 3],  # Reactive power demand (MVAr)\n        'Gs': buses[:, 4],  # Shunt conductance\n        'Bs': buses[:, 5],  # Shunt susceptance\n        'area': buses[:, 6].astype(int),\n        'vm': buses[:, 7],  # Voltage magnitude (initial guess)\n        'va': buses[:, 8],  # Voltage angle (initial guess, degrees)\n        'baseKV': buses[:, 9],  # Base voltage (kV)\n        'zone': buses[:, 10].astype(int),\n        'vmax': buses[:, 11],  # Maximum voltage (pu)\n        'vmin': buses[:, 12],  # Minimum voltage (pu)\n    }\n    return bus_data\n\n\ndef parse_gen_data(generators: np.ndarray, gencosts: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Parse generator data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    n_gen = generators.shape[0]\n    n_cols = generators.shape[1]\n\n    gen_data = {\n        'gen_id': generators[:, 0].astype(int),  # Bus ID\n        'vg': generators[:, 1],  # Voltage magnitude setpoint (pu)\n        'qgen': generators[:, 2],  # Reactive power generation (MVar)\n        'qmax': generators[:, 3],  # Maximum reactive power (MVar)\n        'qmin': generators[:, 4],  # Minimum reactive power (MVar)\n        'pmax': generators[:, 5],  # Maximum active power (MW)\n        'pmin': generators[:, 6],  # Minimum active power (MW)\n        'vmax': generators[:, 7],  # Maximum voltage (pu)\n        'vmin': generators[:, 8],  # Minimum voltage (pu)\n    }\n\n    # Add optional fields if they exist\n    if n_cols > 9:\n        gen_data['ramp_agc'] = generators[:, 9]  # Ramp rate for AGC (MW/min)\n    else:\n        gen_data['ramp_agc'] = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    if n_cols > 10:\n        gen_data['ramp_10'] = generators[:, 10]  # Ramp rate for 10 min (MW)\n    else:\n        gen_data['ramp_10'] = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    if n_cols > 11:\n        gen_data['ramp_q'] = generators[:, 11]  # Ramp rate for reactive (MVar/min)\n    else:\n        gen_data['ramp_q'] = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    if n_cols > 12:\n        gen_data['apf'] = generators[:, 12]  # Area participation factor\n    else:\n        gen_data['apf'] = np.ones(n_gen) / n_gen\n\n    # Parse generator costs (assuming quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    cost_data = {\n        'model': gencosts[:, 0].astype(int),  # Cost model (1=polynomial)\n        'startup': gencosts[:, 1],  # Startup cost\n        'shutdown': gencosts[:, 2],  # Shutdown cost\n        'c0': gencosts[:, 3],  # Constant term\n        'c1': gencosts[:, 4],  # Linear term\n        'c2': gencosts[:, 5],  # Quadratic term\n    }\n\n    return gen_data, cost_data\n\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branches: np.ndarray) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse branch data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    branch_data = {\n        'from_bus': branches[:, 0].astype(int),  # From bus ID\n        'to_bus': branches[:, 1].astype(int),   # To bus ID\n        'r': branches[:, 2],  # Resistance (pu)\n        'x': branches[:, 3],  # Reactance (pu)\n        'b': branches[:, 4],  # Total line charging (pu)\n        'rateA': branches[:, 5],  # Long-term thermal limit (MVA)\n        'rateB': branches[:, 6],  # Short-term thermal limit (MVA)\n        'rateC': branches[:, 7],  # Emergency thermal limit (MVA)\n        'ratio': branches[:, 8],  # Transformer off-nominal turns ratio\n        'angle': branches[:, 9],  # Transformer phase shift angle (degrees)\n        'status': branches[:, 10].astype(int),  # Branch status (1=in service, 0=out)\n        'angmin': branches[:, 11],  # Minimum angle difference (degrees)\n        'angmax': branches[:, 12],  # Maximum angle difference (degrees)\n    }\n    return branch_data\n\n\ndef build_y_matrix(n_bus: int, branch_data: Dict, baseMVA: float) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix from branch data\"\"\"\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Add line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Update Y matrix\n        Y[f, f] += (y + b_half) / (abs(tap) ** 2)\n        Y[t, t] += y + b_half\n        Y[f, t] -= y / ca.conj(tap)\n        Y[t, f] -= y / tap\n\n    return Y\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow problem using CasADi\n    Based on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    gen_data, cost_data = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'], network_data['gencost'])\n    branch_data = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data['bus_id'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data['gen_id'])\n\n    # Find reference bus (type == 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus_data['bus_type'][i] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # If no slack bus, use first generator bus\n        ref_bus_idx = int(gen_data['gen_id'][0]) - 1\n\n    # Build optimization variables\n    # Voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', n_bus)\n    va = ca.SX.sym('va', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', n_gen)\n    qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Combine all variables\n    vars = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n    n_vars = vars.size()[0]\n\n    # Set initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    # Voltage magnitudes\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[i] = bus_data['vm'][i] if bus_data['vm'][i] > 0 else 1.0\n    # Voltage angles\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0[n_bus + i] = np.deg2rad(bus_data['va'][i])\n    # Generator power (use middle of bounds)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        pmin = gen_data['pmin'][k]\n        pmax = gen_data['pmax'][k]\n        x0[2*n_bus + k] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n        qmin = gen_data['qmin'][k]\n        qmax = gen_data['qmax'][k]\n        x0[3*n_bus + k] = (qmin + qmax) / 2.0\n\n    # Build constraints list\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle = 0\n    g = ca.vertcat(g, va[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, pg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['pmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['pmax'][k])\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, qg[k])\n        lbg.append(gen_data['qmin'][k])\n        ubg.append(gen_data['qmax'][k])\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, vm[i])\n        lbg.append(bus_data['vmin'][i])\n        ubg.append(bus_data['vmax'][i])\n\n    # Build Y matrix (as numpy, then convert to CasADi)\n    Y_numpy = build_y_matrix(n_bus, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Convert Y matrix to CasADi\n    Y_re = ca.DM(Y_numpy.real)\n    Y_im = ca.DM(Y_numpy.imag)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate net power injection at bus i\n        Pg_i = 0.0\n        Qg_i = 0.0\n\n        # Sum generators at this bus\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_data['gen_id'][k] == bus_data['bus_id'][i]:\n                Pg_i += pg[k]\n                Qg_i += qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate load (convert from MW/MVAr to pu)\n        Pd_i = bus_data['Pd'][i] / baseMVA\n        Qd_i = bus_data['Qd'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate shunt admittance contribution\n        Gs_i = bus_data['Gs'][i] / baseMVA\n        Bs_i = bus_data['Bs'][i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow equations using rectangular coordinates\n        # Calculate complex voltage\n        V_complex = vm[i] * ca.cos(va[i]) + 1j * vm[i] * ca.sin(va[i])\n\n        # Calculate power injection S = V * conj(I) = V * conj(Y * V)\n        S_calc = 0.0 + 0.0j\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            # Complex voltage at bus j\n            Vj_complex = vm[j] * ca.cos(va[j]) + 1j * vm[j] * ca.sin(va[j])\n\n            # Admittance element\n            Y_ij = Y_re[i, j] + 1j * Y_im[i, j]\n\n            # Add contribution\n            S_calc += V_complex * ca.conj(Y_ij * Vj_complex)\n\n        # Extract real and imaginary parts\n        P_calc = ca.re(S_calc)\n        Q_calc = ca.im(S_calc)\n\n        # Add shunt contribution\n        P_shunt = Gs_i * vm[i]**2\n        Q_shunt = -Bs_i * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Active power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        P_balance = Pg_i - Pd_i - P_shunt - P_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, P_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n        Q_balance = Qg_i - Qd_i - Q_shunt - Q_calc\n        g = ca.vertcat(g, Q_balance)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (apparent power limit)\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf_complex = vm[f_bus] * ca.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * vm[f_bus] * ca.sin(va[f_bus])\n        Vt_complex = vm[t_bus] * ca.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * vm[t_bus] * ca.sin(va[t_bus])\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * ca.exp(1j * ca.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf_complex * ca.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * ca.conj(tap)) * Vf_complex - y / ca.conj(tap) * Vt_complex)\n\n        # Magnitude of complex power flow\n        flow_mva = ca.sqrt(ca.re(Sf)**2 + ca.im(Sf)**2)\n\n        # Apply MVA limit\n        if branch_data['rateA'][k] > 0:\n            g = ca.vertcat(g, flow_mva)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(branch_data['rateA'][k])\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    J = 0.0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        J += cost_data['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + cost_data['c1'][k] * pg[k] + cost_data['c0'][k]\n\n    # Create solver\n    prob = {'x': vars, 'f': J, 'g': g}\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 2000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        },\n        'expand': True\n    }\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', prob, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=None, ubx=None, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Check solution status\n    status = solver.stats()['return_status']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    sol_vars = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n    vm_sol = sol_vars[0:n_bus]\n    va_sol = sol_vars[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    pg_sol = sol_vars[2*n_bus:3*n_bus]\n    qg_sol = sol_vars[3*n_bus:4*n_bus]\n\n    return {\n        'status': status,\n        'vm': vm_sol,\n        'va': va_sol,\n        'pg': pg_sol,\n        'qg': qg_sol,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch flows and return most loaded branches\"\"\"\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n    branch_data = solution['branch_data']\n    baseMVA = solution['baseMVA']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(len(branch_data['from_bus'])):\n        if branch_data['status'][k] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]) - 1\n        t_bus = int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]) - 1\n\n        # Complex voltages\n        Vf = vm[f_bus] * (np.cos(va[f_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[f_bus]))\n        Vt = vm[t_bus] * (np.cos(va[t_bus]) + 1j * np.sin(va[t_bus]))\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data['r'][k]\n        x = branch_data['x'][k]\n        b = branch_data['b'][k]\n        ratio = branch_data['ratio'][k]\n        angle = branch_data['angle'][k]\n\n        # Convert to admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Handle transformers\n        if ratio == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n        else:\n            tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * np.deg2rad(angle))\n\n        # Line charging\n        b_half = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Calculate complex power flow from f to t\n        Sf = Vf * np.conj((y + b_half) / (tap * np.conj(tap)) * Vf - y / np.conj(tap) * Vt)\n        St = Vt * np.conj((y + b_half) * Vt - y / tap * Vf)\n\n        # Magnitudes\n        flow_from_MVA = np.sqrt(Sf.real**2 + Sf.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_MVA = np.sqrt(St.real**2 + St.imag**2) * baseMVA\n        limit_MVA = branch_data['rateA'][k]\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n        if limit_MVA > 0:\n            loading_pct = (flow_from_MVA / limit_MVA) * 100.0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(branch_data['from_bus'][k]),\n            'to_bus': int(branch_data['to_bus'][k]),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_MVA),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_MVA),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_MVA)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading percentage (descending) and take top 10\n    branch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    return branch_flows[:10]\n\n\ndef generate_report(solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    # Calculate totals\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(solution['bus_data']['Pd'])\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(solution['bus_data']['Qd'])\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(solution['pg'] * solution['baseMVA'])\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(solution['qg'] * solution['baseMVA'])\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Bus ID\n        'vg': generators[:, 1],  # Voltage magnitude setpoint (pu)\n        'qgen': generators[:, 2],  # Reactive power generation (MVar)\n        'qmax': generators[:, 3],  # Maximum reactive power (MVar)\n        'qmin': generators[:, 4],  # Minimum reactive power (MVar)\n        'pmax': generators[:, 5],  # Maximum active power (MW)\n        'pmin': generators[:, 6],  # Minimum active power (MW)\n        'vmax': generators[:, 7],  # Maximum voltage (pu)\n        'vmin': generators[:, 8],  # Minimum voltage (pu)\n    }\n\n    # Add optional fields if they exist\n    if n_cols > 9:\n        gen_data['ramp_agc'] = generators[:, 9]  # Ramp rate for AGC (MW/min)\n    else:\n        gen_data['ramp_agc'] = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    if n_cols > 10:\n        gen_data['ramp_10'] = generators[:, 10]  #
